Motivation: 现有网球视频方法存在"感知与理解割裂"的问题：要么仅识别单个击球动作而不建模其战术依赖关系，要么只生成高层分析却缺乏底层事件支撑。作者旨在弥合这一 gap，使战术解释能够锚定在具体的击球事件上。

Method: 1) 定义新任务"stroke-evidence-grounded tactical reasoning"，要求模型联合预测开放式答案、层级战术标签、有序支撑击球序列及决定性关键动作，且每条证据击球锚定到球拍触球帧。2) 构建 TRACE 基准：包含 11,189 段回合视频、41,485 个击球事件、25,429 个战术单元和 11,189 对问答，统一了细粒度击球属性、跨击球战术关系、层级战术标注，以及覆盖事实感知、战术理解与决策推理的证据锚定问题。3) 提出 TennisVAR，一个证据锚定的多模态大语言模型，遵循"事件-关系-证据-战术"推理范式：事件解析模块将连续回合转换为显式击球事件序列，战术图引导的时序推理器联合建模回合进程与同一球员的决策依赖，以定位与问题相关的证据和决定性动作。

Result: 构建了 TRACE 大规模专家标注基准（11,189 回合视频、41,485 击球事件、25,429 战术单元、11,189 问答对），并提出 TennisVAR 模型，通过事件解析与战术图引导时序推理实现证据锚定的战术推理。

Conclusion: 通过引入证据锚定任务、构建 TRACE 基准并设计遵循"事件-关系-证据-战术"范式的 TennisVAR 模型，本文实现了网球视频从单一击球感知到可解释战术理解与推理的跨越，为后续研究提供了统一的评测基准与基线方法。

Abstract: Sports-video understanding is moving beyond event recognition toward explaining how actions collectively shape match progression, however, existing tennis-video methods either perceive individual strokes without modeling their tactical dependencies or generate high-level analyses without grounding them in the underlying events. To bridge this perception-to-understanding gap, we formulate stroke-evidence-grounded tactical reasoning, a new rally-level task that requires models to jointly predict an open-ended answer, a hierarchical tactic label, an ordered sequence of supporting strokes, and decisive key actions, with each evidence stroke anchored to its racket-ball contact frame. We further introduce TRACE (Tactical Reasoning with Action-Chain Evidence in Tennis), a large-scale expert-annotated benchmark containing 11,189 rally videos, 41,485 stroke events, 25,429 tactical units, and 11,189 question-answer pairs, which unifies fine-grained stroke attributes, cross-stroke tactical relations, hierarchical tactic annotations, and evidence-grounded questions across factual perception, tactical understanding, and decision reasoning. Building on TRACE, we propose TennisVAR (Tennis Video Action-chain Reasoner), an evidence-grounded multimodal large language model that follows an "event-relation-evidence-tactic" reasoning paradigm, where an Event Parsing Module converts continuous rallies into explicit stroke-event sequences while a Tactical Graph-Guided Temporal Reasoner jointly models rally progression and same-player decision dependencies to identify question-relevant evidence and decisive actions.

</details>


### [34] [A Deep RL based Framework for Targeted White Matter Tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12960)
*Ankita Joshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需真实纤维标注的混合框架，将强化学习与监督学习（GPT策略学习）结合以优化面向特定纤维束的纤维束成像策略，并进一步提出可扩展的多策略融合框架，通过多个RL策略的互补优势提升纤维束成像的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维束成像在神经影像与临床应用中至关重要，但白质结构复杂、易产生假阳性，导致关键通路被错误重建；同时传统方法依赖真实纤维标注、需要显式分割流程，限制了泛化性与实用性。因此需要一个不依赖ground-truth、面向特定纤维束且更鲁棒的框架。

Method: 提出面向特定纤维束（tract-specific）的混合框架，将强化学习与监督学习（基于GPT的策略学习）结合以细化RL策略，无需真实纤维标注，并绕过显式分割步骤；在此基础上进一步提出可扩展的数据驱动多策略融合框架，利用多个RL策略的互补优势提升性能。

Result: 在TractoInferno、HCP和ISMRM-2015等公开基准数据集上进行了广泛验证，结果表明该框架能够跨数据源泛化并准确重建脑白质纤维束，在不依赖ground-truth标注的情况下提升了准确性、鲁棒性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合RL-监督学习策略及多策略融合框架代表了纤维束成像领域的显著进步，在减少对ground-truth依赖的同时，提高了方法的鲁棒性、可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: Fiber tractography's ability to reconstruct the brain's structural pathways, has made it a crucial component of modern neuroimaging, enabling detailed, non-invasive mapping of structural connectivity and supporting a wide range of neurological research and clinical applications. However, despite its importance, tractography remains a challenging task due to the inherent complexity of white matter structure and its susceptibility to false positives, which can lead to the misrepresentation of critical pathways.
  To overcome these limitations, in this thesis, we propose a hybrid framework that integrates reinforcement learning with supervised learning for refining RL policies, specifically tailored for tract-specific tractography. Notably, our framework does not rely on ground-truth fibers for training. Moreover, the tract-specific formulation bypasses the need for an explicit segmentation process, simplifying the overall pipeline.
  Our work includes two main contributions, each building upon the previous. First, we introduce a hybrid approach that combines reinforcement learning with supervised learning (specifically, GPT-based policy learning) to refine policies in a tract-specific context. Second, we propose a scalable framework for data-driven multi-policy fusion, which leverages the complementary strengths of multiple RL policies to improve tractography performance and robustness. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework through extensive validation on benchmark public datasets including TractoInferno, HCP, and ISMRM-2015, highlighting its ability to generalize across data sources and accurately reconstruct brain white matter tracts. We believe that these contributions represent significant advancements in the field of tractography, improving robustness, reliability, and accuracy while reducing dependence on ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [35] [Bias Mitigation in Face Recognition via Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12971)
*Yu Linghu,Salman Mohammad,Xinyi Zhang,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向人口统计公平的人脸识别损失函数 DeSCon（Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive loss），通过精心设计训练批次组成与人口感知的样本对选择，改善非匹配分数分布尾部的公平性，而非仅依赖类别平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统在不同性别、年龄、种族等人口统计群体上表现出不同的错误率（偏差）。虽然训练数据在人口统计维度上不平衡是偏差的一个原因，但人工平衡训练集并不能完全消除该问题。实际部署中人脸识别通常工作在极低误匹配率的操作点上，即关注非匹配分数分布的尾部，而类别均衡只能改善分布均值，无法有效处理尾部行为。

Method: 提出 DeSCon（Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive loss）人脸识别损失函数。该方法通过精心设计的训练批次组成和人口感知（demographic-aware）的样本对选择策略，直接针对非匹配分数分布的尾部进行优化，从而提升公平性。

Result: 在带有人口统计标签的数据集和标准验证基准上的实验表明，DeSCon 在保持有竞争力的验证性能的同时，能够在训练集平衡的基础上进一步提升公平性。

Conclusion: 单纯平衡训练数据不足以解决人脸识别的偏差问题，需要针对低误匹配率下的分数分布尾部进行优化。DeSCon 通过人口感知的对比学习提供了有效的公平性改进方案，同时不牺牲验证性能。

Abstract: Face recognition systems have been shown to be biased toward certain demographic groups by exhibiting different error rates across gender, age, or ethnicity. Though the imbalance of the training data with respect to these demographics is one cause of this bias, training on artificially balanced groups does not completely mitigate the problem. For deployment, face recognition typically works at operating points allowing very low false match rates and, hence, on the tail of the non-match score distribution. While class balancing can improve the means of these distributions, the aim of our approach is to improve fairness by addressing the behavior in the tail. Particularly, we propose the Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive loss (DeSCon) for face recognition, which relies on a well-designed composition of training batches and demographic-aware pair selection. Our experimental evaluation on both demographically-labeled datasets and standard verification benchmarks shows that DeSCon can improve fairness beyond balancing training datasets while maintaining competitive verification performance. Source code is available upon request.

</details>


### [36] [DiCoR: Decoupled Referent Disambiguation and Contour Recalibration for Efficient Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12980)
*Ziyang Gao,Zhizhuo Jiang,Jingjing Chang,Yixin Yang,Yuwen Pan,Yong-Qiang Mao,Yu Liu,Hai-Bao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiCoR 提出一种解耦的指代消歧与轮廓校准框架，在保持高效 JFS 管线的基础上，通过指代消歧定位引导策略和轻量轮廓校准模块，在 RefSegRS、RRSIS-D 和 RISBench 三个基准上取得最优分割精度，并展现出良好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像指代分割方法分为联合融合分割（JFS）与解耦提示分割（DPS）两类。JFS 效率高但精度受限（定位与掩码分割在同一目标下优化），DPS 精度高但内存开销和推理延迟大。本文希望弥合这一差距，在保持 JFS 效率的同时提升分割精度。

Method: 提出 DiCoR，一种构建于高效 JFS 管线之上的解耦指代消歧与轮廓校准框架。包括两部分：1）消歧感知定位引导策略，利用自适应语言线索对显著候选区域排序，并将得到的定位先验注入融合特征；2）轻量轮廓校准模块，在局部轮廓监督下对粗分割 logits 预测残差修正，以有限计算开销提升掩码质量。

Result: 在 RefSegRS、RRSIS-D 和 RISBench 三个基准上均取得最优分割精度。在 RefSegRS 上，相比一个有竞争力的 JFS 方法，mIoU 提升 5.28%、gIoU 提升 2.87%，同时比代表性 DPS 方法快 4.7%，展示了良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: DiCoR 通过解耦指代消歧与轮廓校准，在高效 JFS 管线上显著提升遥感图像指代分割精度，并与 DPS 方法相比具备速度优势，实现精度与效率的良好平衡。代码已开源。

Abstract: Referring remote sensing image segmentation (RRSIS) aims to delineate targets specified by natural language expressions in remote sensing imagery. Existing methods mainly follow joint fusion segmentation (JFS) or decoupled prompt segmentation (DPS). JFS is efficient but often suffers from limited accuracy because referent localization and mask delineation are optimized under a unified objective, whereas DPS separates localization from mask generation using spatial prompts and foundation segmenters at the cost of higher memory consumption and inference latency. To bridge this gap, we propose DiCoR, a decoupled referent disambiguation and contour recalibration framework built on an efficient JFS pipeline. DiCoR addresses two key challenges: distinguishing the correct referent from ambiguous candidates and refining coarse masks after localization. A disambiguation-aware localization guidance strategy ranks salient candidate regions with adaptive linguistic cues and injects the resulting localization prior into fused features. A lightweight contour recalibration module further predicts residual corrections to coarse logits under localized contour supervision, improving mask quality with limited computational overhead. Experiments on RefSegRS, RRSIS-D, and RISBench show that DiCoR achieves the best segmentation accuracy across all three benchmarks. On RefSegRS, it improves mIoU and gIoU by 5.28% and 2.87% over a competitive JFS method while running 4.7% faster than a representative DPS method, demonstrating a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/zyGao1126/DiCoR.

</details>


### [37] [PixSDS: Why Latent SDS Makes Noisy Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12997)
*Vsevolod Skorokhodov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文诊断了潜在分数蒸馏采样（latent SDS）在文本到3D生成中产生彩色伪影与高频纹理噪声的成因——VAE引起的像素漂移，并提出PixSDS这一轻量的VAE一致性梯度修复方法，在无需重新训练模型、更换渲染器或替换SDS目标的情况下显著减少伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在SDS虽然推动了文本到3D生成的发展，但常出现结构化颜色伪影和高频纹理噪声。作者旨在找出这一失败模式的根本原因，并提出实用、低成本的解决方案。

Method: 作者首先通过受控2D SDS实验、仅VAE优化以及简化分析，证明失败源于VAE引起的像素漂移：优化图像会沿VAE编码器弱约束的像素空间方向移动，导致潜空间表示依然干净而图像却累积可见伪影。基于此提出PixSDS——一种轻量VAE一致性梯度修复方法：它解码一个潜在SDS的“前瞻”（lookahead）步骤，并将解码后的图像作为像素空间优化的干净方向，从而减少VAE不一致方向上的运动。

Result: 在2D优化和文本到3D生成实验中，PixSDS在保持语义内容的同时大幅减少了结构化伪影。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 潜在SDS的伪影源于VAE诱导的像素漂移，而PixSDS通过轻量的VAE一致性梯度修复有效缓解了该问题，无需重训扩散模型、改变渲染器或更换SDS目标，为文本到3D生成提供了一种实用改进。

Abstract: Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) enables text-to-3D generation by optimizing rendered images with a pretrained diffusion prior, but latent SDS often produces structured color artifacts and high-frequency texture noise. We identify a failure mode of latent SDS caused by VAE-induced pixel drift: the optimized image can move along pixel-space directions that are weakly constrained by the VAE encoder, so its latent representation remains clean and semantically meaningful while the image itself accumulates visible artifacts. We support this diagnosis with controlled 2D SDS experiments, VAE-only optimization, and a simplified analysis showing that encoder-like latent objectives can amplify image-space noise when the inverse mapping to pixels is underconstrained. Motivated by this observation, we propose PixSDS, a lightweight VAE-consistent gradient repair method. PixSDS decodes a latent SDS lookahead step and uses the decoded image as a clean direction for pixel-space optimization, reducing motion in VAE-inconsistent directions without retraining the diffusion model, changing the renderer, or replacing the SDS objective. Experiments in 2D optimization and text-to-3D generation show that PixSDS substantially reduces structured artifacts while preserving semantic content. Code is publicly available at https://sevashasla.github.io/pixsds-webpage/.

</details>


### [38] [Structure-aware Riemannian Growth Fields for 4D Plant Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13007)
*Meng-Yu Jennifer Kuo,Ryo Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种4D植物生长建模框架，在结构感知的黎曼生长场上将植物形态发生建模为连续程序化过程，从稀疏时序观测重建植物的连续几何与拓扑演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D植物建模方法依赖稠密配准，但因扫描约束和自遮挡难以获得可靠的稠密序列；在大时间间隔下，器官快速萌发破坏了局部刚性假设，导致这些方法失效。因此需要一个能在稀疏观测和拓扑剧变下仍保持连续生长建模的方案。

Method: 将植物形态发生表述为结构感知的黎曼生长场上的连续程序化过程，联合建模拓扑演化与几何形变，保留植物学层级和跨时间点的稳定时空对应；核心思想是将符号化生长规则嵌入连续测地流，使器官发育沿生物调制的轨迹进行，在拓扑变化下保持结构一致性。

Result: 作者贡献了一个10天双物种数据集，含稠密几何与语义标注；实验表明该方法能精确追踪单个器官随时间的生长，并在几何精度和对应一致性上显著优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过将植物生长建模为连续程序化过程并在黎曼生长场上联合处理拓扑与几何，该方法有效解决了稀疏观测与拓扑剧变下的4D建模难题，验证了其在器官追踪与对应一致性上的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel framework for 4D plant growth modeling that reconstructs the continuous geometric and topological evolution of plants from sparse temporal observations. Existing methods mainly rely on dense registration, yet reliable dense sequences are hard to obtain due to scanning constraints and self-occlusions, leaving these approaches struggling under large temporal gaps where rapid organ emergence violates local rigidity. To overcome this, we bridge these gaps by formulating plant morphogenesis as a continuous procedural process on a structure-aware Riemannian growth field; this jointly models topology evolution and geometric deformation, preserving botanical hierarchies and stable spatio-temporal correspondences across distant timepoints. Our key idea is to ground symbolic growth rules within a continuous geodesic flow, where organ development follows biologically modulated trajectories that preserve structural coherence under topological changes. We further contribute a 10-day dual-species dataset with dense geometric and semantic annotations. Experiments demonstrate that our method accurately tracks individual organ growth over time and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric accuracy and correspondence consistency.

</details>


### [39] [EgoPHI: Estimating Contact and Force from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13014)
*Andela Ilic,Rachel Schuchert,Yijing Jiang,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoPHI 是首个从单目 RGB 图像和物体几何信息中联合估计手部与物体网格上密集接触图与 3D 力分布的方法，并通过物理仿真管线和真实物体实验验证了其泛化与 sim-to-real 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以自我为中心的手-物交互理解大多停留在接触定位层面，缺乏对作用在手上和物体上的力的估计，无法实现真正基于物理的交互推理。同时，缺乏可扩展的力真值标注制约了此类研究。

Method: 提出 EgoPHI，从单张单目 RGB 图像与物体几何出发，在交互的手部和关节化物体网格上联合估计密集 3D 接触与力分布。为获得可扩展的监督信号，设计了基于物理的仿真管线，为现有手-物数据集补充逐顶点力标注；并通过构建两个能采集密集接触与力幅值的实体物体、由八名参与者记录多样触摸与抓握交互，验证 sim-to-real 迁移。

Result: 在分布内与分布外基准上，EgoPHI 的力估计优于现有方法，并能泛化到未见数据集；在真实世界自建数据集上，EgoPHI 恢复了有意义的 3D 接触与力分布，验证了仿真、分布外和真实场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: EgoPHI 将以自我为中心的手-物理解从接触定位推进到基于物理的交互推理，推动了密集 3D 接触与力估计的发展，并展示了良好的跨数据集泛化与真实世界适用性。

Abstract: Understanding hand-object interaction from egocentric vision is essential for modeling how people physically engage with the surrounding world. Yet reasoning about physically grounded interaction requires estimating the forces acting on hands and objects, beyond localizing contact. We present EgoPHI, the first method that jointly estimates dense contact maps and 3D force distributions on hand and object meshes from a single monocular RGB image and object geometry. To address the lack of scalable ground-truth force annotations, we introduce a physics-based simulation pipeline that augments existing hand-object datasets with dense per-vertex force supervision. EgoPHI then learns dense 3D contact and force on interacting hand and articulated object meshes, extending vision-based force estimation beyond image-space or planar settings. Our evaluation on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks shows that EgoPHI improves force estimation over existing approaches while generalizing to unseen datasets. To evaluate sim-to-real transfer, we constructed two physical objects that capture dense object contact and force magnitude and used them to record a dataset of interactions from eight participants across diverse touch and grasp types. Our results demonstrate that EgoPHI recovers meaningful 3D contact and force distributions in simulated, out-of-distribution, and real-world settings, advancing egocentric hand-object understanding from contact localization toward physically grounded interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [40] [RGB-D Video Generation for Improving Human-to-Robot Object Handover Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13028)
*Tianyu Sun,Zhoujie Fu,Zihui Gao,Bang Zhang,Guosheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对人机交接场景数据稀缺与仿真-现实差距问题，本文提出Hand2Bot数据集与PassGen生成管线，结合深度编辑策略实现高精度意图识别与低误触发，支持零样本迁移与更早的意图预判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机物体交接是协作的核心能力，但大规模以人为中心的数据集稀缺，且仿真到现实的差距显著，制约了相关研究进展。

Method: 提出Hand2Bot RGB-D视频数据集，采集交接场景中的身体姿态与面部表情等上下文信息；设计PassGen生成管线，利用稳定视频扩散和意图感知时序面部编码器合成真实交接序列并保证手-物一致性；采用基于形态学的深度编辑策略模拟物理深度图的真实传感器噪声，以弥合仿真-现实差距。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在消融研究和实体机器人部署中均取得高意图识别准确率和低误触发率；与传统的以手为中心的基线相比，基于PassGen的训练可实现鲁棒的零样本迁移和更早的意图预判。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效弥合仿真-现实差距，实现高准确率、低误触发的意图识别，并支持零样本迁移与提前预判，从而在共享工作空间中实现具有社会感知能力的机器人行为。

Abstract: Human-to-robot (H2R) object handover is a fundamental capability for human-robot collaboration, yet progress is hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, human-centric datasets and the significant sim-to-real gap. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand2Bot, an RGB-D video dataset that provides rich contextual information such as body posture and facial expressions, specifically collected for handover scenarios with real-world noise patterns. We further propose PassGen, a generative pipeline that leverages stable video diffusion and an Intention-Aware Temporal Face Encoder to synthesize realistic handover sequences while ensuring hand-object consistency. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a morphology-based depth editing strategy that replicates realistic sensor noise found in physical depth maps. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves high intention identification accuracy and low false trigger rates in both ablation studies and real-world deployment on a physical robot platform. Our results confirm that training on PassGen allows for robust zero-shot transfer and earlier intention anticipation compared to traditional hand-centric baselines, effectively enabling socially aware robotic behavior in shared workspaces.

</details>


### [41] [UniTraffic-Agent: Unified Traffic Video Reasoning for AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3 with Two Out-of-Domain Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13031)
*Peng Li,Qianqian Xu,Shilong Bao,Yangbangyan Jiang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍 UniTraffic-Agent，一个用于交通视频理解的智能体系统，在 AI City Challenge Track 3 的 TAR 及两个外域评测（FETV、PSI-VQA）中均取得靠前排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通视频理解是智能交通的重要问题，视频能直接揭示事故、违规及车辆与弱势道路使用者间的交互。理想系统需解释事件如何发展、为何发生及交互何时出现，但由于交通视频事件稀疏、视角多样，现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）难以胜任。

Method: 提出 UniTraffic-Agent，采用 observe–reason–act–verify 工作流：采样带时间戳的视觉证据；在同一次请求中对同一片段的所有问题进行推理；并通过任务特定的 action adapters 转换响应。该系统是第 10 届 AI City Challenge Track 3 的 MR-CAS 方案。

Result: 在官方公开排行榜上，MR-CAS 在 TAR 上排名第 16（得分 0.5780），在 FETV 上排名第 2（0.4884），在 PSI-VQA 上排名第 4（64.4161）。

Conclusion: UniTraffic-Agent 通过观察-推理-行动-验证的智能体工作流，有效处理稀疏事件与多变视角的交通视频，在多个交通视频理解任务上取得有竞争力的成绩，代码已开源。

Abstract: Traffic video understanding has become an important problem in intelligent transportation, as road videos provide direct evidence for accidents, violations, and interactions between vehicles and vulnerable road users. A useful system should explain how a traffic event develops, why it happens, and when the relevant interaction occurs, yet this remains difficult for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) because traffic videos contain sparse events and varied viewpoints. We introduce UniTraffic-Agent, the MR-CAS solution for Track~3 of the 10th AI City Challenge, which includes Traffic Anomaly Reasoning (TAR) and two out-of-domain evaluations: FETV for fisheye traffic events and PSI-VQA for pedestrian intention reasoning. UniTraffic-Agent follows an observe--reason--act--verify workflow that samples timestamped visual evidence, reasons over all questions from the same clip in one request, and converts responses through task-specific action adapters. On the official Public leaderboards, MR-CAS ranks 16th on TAR with a score of 0.5780, 2nd on FETV with 0.4884, and 4th on PSI-VQA with 64.4161. The code is available at https://github.com/Roclp/UniTraffic-Agent.

</details>


### [42] [Spatially-Grounded Text-to-Video Generation via Inference-Time Gradient-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13037)
*Guillaume Jeanneret,Mathis Koroglu,Hugo Caselles-Dupré,Arnaud Dapogny,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GATO-Vid，一种免训练、免梯度的空间可控文本到视频生成方法，通过对交叉注意力分数解析求解获得闭式解，显著提升定位精度且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散 Transformer 文本到视频模型虽合成质量高，但细粒度空间可控性仍是难题；现有免训练方法虽有较好整体效果，却依赖基于梯度的优化，计算开销巨大，在大规模现代架构中尤为突出。

Method: 提出 GATO-Vid：摒弃昂贵的反向传播，引入一种替代的交叉注意力分数并解析求解，得到精确闭式解；同时设计一种针对 Transformer 潜在空间拓扑流形的即时（on-the-fly）注入机制以应用该解析解。

Result: 实验表明 GATO-Vid 在定位精度上显著优于现有基线方法，同时仅引入极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: GATO-Vid 通过免梯度、解析的方式实现了精确的空间引导，在保持高合成质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，为文本到视频的空间可控生成提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformer Text-to-Video models have achieved remarkable synthesis quality, yet fine-grained spatial controllability remains a significant challenge. While existing training-free methods produce solid overall results in spatially grounded generation, \ie, placing a specific object in a designated location, they rely on gradient-based optimization techniques that incur prohibitive computational overhead, a bottleneck amplified in modern large-scale architectures. To address this limitation, we present Gradient-free Analytical Trajectory Optimization Video Generation (GATO-Vid), a novel training-free and gradient-free approach for precise spatial guidance. Rather than relying on costly backward passes, we introduce an alternative cross-attention score and solve it analytically to obtain an exact, closed-form solution. To use our analytical solution, we propose an on-the-fly injection mechanism tailored to the topological manifold of the transformer's latent space. Our experiments demonstrate that GATO-Vid significantly outperforms existing baselines in localization accuracy while introducing minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [43] [P2Fusion: Prompt-based Progressive Infrared-Visible Image Fusion via Dual-Prior Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13045)
*Yi Shi,Huichao Xie,Yuqing Wang,Mingyu Wang,Kaihui Yang,Yu Liu,Ruitao Lu,Lizhe Li,Junwei Han,Dingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 P2Fusion，一种基于先验引导蒸馏的红外-可见光图像融合框架，通过双内在提示（热显著性、空间质量）蒸馏为可学习的动态调节器，并配合 Teach-to-Fuse 机制与 Gated Dynamic Expert Recalibration（GDER）模块，自适应协调模态竞争，在多个数据集上取得 SOTA 融合质量与下游检测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外-可见光图像融合（IVIF）中，热特征与纹理特征存在固有信息差异，难以协调。现有先验引导方法要么采用静态约束引发优化冲突，要么依赖 CLIP/DINO 等大模型的外部语义先验，未能充分挖掘模态内在特征，难以实现高保真融合。

Method: 提出 P2Fusion 框架：将图像内在先验（热显著性与空间质量）蒸馏为可学习的动态调节器，而非施加硬编码惩罚；设计 Teach-to-Fuse 机制提供双粒度渐进式引导；结合 Gated Dynamic Expert Recalibration（GDER）模块实现解耦的特征细化，通过专家特化自适应地调节模态竞争。

Result: 在 5 个主流数据集上达到 SOTA：在 5 个基准的 20 项关键评价指标中有 14 项取得最优；下游感知鲁棒性提升明显，目标检测 mAP 分别提升 +3.2%（MSRS）、+0.5%（M3FD）、+0.9%（DroneVehicle）。

Conclusion: P2Fusion 通过双内在先验蒸馏与专家特化的动态调节机制，有效解决了红外与可见光模态的信息差异与优化冲突问题，在融合质量和下游感知任务上均表现优异，证明了内在先验引导方法的有效性。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) is pivotal for multimodal perception, yet reconciling the inherent information disparity between thermal and textural features remains a fundamental challenge. Existing prior-guided methods often rely on static constraints that induce optimization conflicts or utilize extrinsic semantic priors from large-scale foundation models (e.g., CLIP/DINO), which frequently fail to exploit the intrinsic modality characteristics essential for high-fidelity fusion. To address these issues, we propose P2Fusion, a prior-guided distillation-based framework that reformulates IVIF via dual intrinsic prompts. Instead of imposing hard-coded penalties, we distill image-intrinsic priors, thermal saliency and spatial quality, into learnable dynamic regulators. Specifically, a Teach-to-Fuse mechanism provides dual-granularity progressive guidance, coupled with a Gated Dynamic Expert Recalibration (GDER) module for decoupled feature refinement. This design enables the network to adaptively mediate modal competition through expert specialization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that P2Fusion achieves state-of-the-art performance across five mainstream datasets. Notably, our framework demonstrates consistent performance advantages in fusion quality, achieving state-of-the-art results in 14 out of 20 key evaluation metrics across 5 benchmarks. Furthermore, it effectively contributes to the robustness of downstream perception, such as +3.2% mAP on MSRS, +0.5% mAP on M3FD and +0.9% mAP on DroneVehicle for object detection. Our code will be available at https://github.com/YiShi99/P2Fusion

</details>


### [44] [Topology-Unified 2D Pose Estimation across Intact, Residual and Prosthetic Limbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13047)
*Tianye Qi,Tengyue Zhang,Jiaying Ying,Tianqing Zhu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProPose 基准与 ProLoss 目标函数，通过统一的拓扑标注协议和真实到合成的数据扩展流程，解决人体姿态估计中对假肢及肢体缺失的表示偏差问题，提升长尾假肢关节分类精度，同时不牺牲空间定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流人体姿态估计（HPE）基准存在严重的表示偏差，几乎只包含健全个体。少数先行数据集虽尝试覆盖肢体差异，但其标注协议难以泛化，无法表示跑步刀锋、未装假肢的残肢等专门机械结构。因此需要统一的拓扑表示框架和充足的数据来解决长尾分布下的假肢姿态估计难题。

Method: 作者提出两大贡献：（1）ProPose——大规模基准，采用新型标注协议，在单一框架内统一生物肢体、多样化假肢与肢体物理缺失的拓扑表示；并设计 Real-to-Synthetic 数据扩展流程，针对长尾的欠代表样本进行显式合成与扩充。（2）ProLoss——结构感知的目标函数，强制同一肢体内部关键点之间的依赖关系，防止模型在机械结构上幻觉出不存在的关节，从而避免出现不切实际的肢体预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法将长尾假肢关节的分类准确率提升了 2% 至 6%，同时未损害空间坐标定位性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为包容性姿态估计奠定了基础，为人机身体与辅助设备交互的理解开辟了新可能。

Abstract: Driven by the availability of large-scale datasets, Human Pose Estimation (HPE) plays a critical role in numerous downstream tasks. However, mainstream benchmarks exhibit severe representation bias, predominantly featuring able-bodied individuals. While a few pioneering datasets have attempted to address limb differences, their annotation protocols fail to generalize, struggling to represent specialized mechanical structures like running blades or unprosthetized residual limbs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ProPose, a large-scale benchmark featuring a novel annotation protocol that unifies the topological representation of biological limbs, diverse prostheses, and physical absences within a single framework. Because real-world prosthetic images are inherently scarce and exhibit extreme long-tail distributions, we design a Real-to-Synthetic data expansion pipeline to explicitly synthesize and expand the underrepresented cases. However, simply training existing models on this enriched dataset often leads to suboptimal solutions, as they estimate each keypoint independently and might hallucinate non-existent joints on mechanical structures. To resolve this, we propose ProLoss, a structure-aware objective that enforces keypoint dependencies within a single limb to prevent unrealistic limb predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach improves the classification accuracy of long-tail prosthetic joints by 2% to 6% without compromising spatial coordinate localization performance. This work sets a foundation for inclusive pose estimation, unlocking new possibilities for understanding the interactions between human bodies and assistive devices.

</details>


### [45] [Learning Unified Video and Image Representation for Video Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13064)
*Haotian Liu,Yang Liu,Guoying Zhao,Xiaobai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UVIF 框架，利用额外标注的静态图像为视频提供细粒度监督，通过统一编码器和多任务学习联合建模视频与图像，实现部分伪造视频的精准检测且无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频人脸伪造检测方法通常假设伪造视频的所有帧都被篡改，而在仅部分帧被篡改的部分伪造视频检测上表现不佳。作者旨在利用额外标注图像提供的细粒度监督来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出 UVIF 框架：1）采用带时域融合模块的 2D 主干作为统一编码器，以多任务学习范式联合建模视频与图像；2）设计伪标签生成过程，将视频帧的表征与静态图像表征建立关联；3）引入面向视频的特征对齐策略，缩小视频与图像之间的分布差距。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，UVIF 在检测部分伪造视频方面优于最先进方法，同时不引入额外的计算开销。

Conclusion: UVIF 通过统一编码器、伪标签生成与视频-图像特征对齐，有效提升了对部分伪造视频的检测性能，超越了现有最先进方法，且保持计算效率不变。代码已开源。

Abstract: Face forgery detection is crucial for preserving the security and integrity of facial data given the rapid developments in face manipulation techniques and deep generative models. Existing methods for video face forgery detection typically assume that all frames in a forged video are manipulated, while detecting partially forged videos that contain only a subset of altered frames remains challenging. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework, UVIF, that utilizes additional annotated images to provide fine-grained supervision for detecting partial forgeries in videos. UVIF employs a unified encoder and a multi-task learning paradigm to jointly model facial videos and images for boosted video face forgery detection. A 2D backbone with temporal fusion modules is employed as the unified encoder. A pseudo labeling process is designed for video frames to bridge their representations with those of static images. A video-oriented feature alignment strategy is further introduced to reduce the distribution gap between videos and images. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework, which outperforms state-of-theart methods in detecting partially forged videos while introducing no additional computational overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/haotianll/UVIF.

</details>


### [46] [Paths: Prompt-aware Spatio-temporal Transformer with Hierarchical Multi-modal Fusion for RGB-Event Video Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13092)
*Yakun Huo,Yingquan Wang,Yangyang Liu,Tianyu Yan,Yunzhi Zhuge,Pingping Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Paths，一个面向 RGB-事件视频行人重识别（RE-VReID）的统一框架，通过记忆增强主干、提示感知时空 Transformer 和分层多模态融合，联合建模时空关系并融合全局与局部多模态特征，在三个公开基准上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RE-VReID 方法存在两个主要问题：一是空间与时间建模相互解耦，限制了二者的交互；二是仅在全局层面进行 RGB-事件融合，无法充分挖掘细粒度判别线索。因此需要一种能统一时空建模并进行分层多模态融合的方法。

Method: 提出 Paths 框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 记忆增强主干（MAB），维护模态特定的身份原型以稳定模态内表示学习；(2) 提示感知时空 Transformer（PST），在统一 Transformer 中联合建模空间与时间线索；(3) 分层多模态融合（HMF），在全局与局部两个层面整合 RGB 与事件特征。

Result: 在 EvReID、MARS 和 iLIDS-VID 三个公开 RE-VReID 基准上进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过 MAB、PST 与 HMF 三个模块，Paths 能学习鲁棒且具有判别力的表示，有效解决 RE-VReID 中的时空解耦与粗粒度融合问题，实验证明了其优越性，代码已开源。

Abstract: RGB-Event Video Person Re-Identification (RE-VReID) aims to retrieve specific person across non-overlapping cameras with complementary RGB videos and event streams. However, existing methods often decouple spatial and temporal modeling, which limits their interaction. In addition, global-level RGB-Event fusion fails to fully exploit fine-grained discriminative cues. To address these issues, we propose Paths, a unified framework with spatio-temporal modeling and hierarchical multi-modal fusion for RE-VReID. Specifically, we first design a Memory-Augmented Backbone (MAB) to maintain modality-specific identity prototypes for stable intra-modal representation learning. Then, we propose a Prompt-aware Spatio-temporal Transformer (PST) to jointly model spatial and temporal cues within a unified Transformer. Finally, we introduce a Hierarchical Multi-modal Fusion (HMF) to integrate RGB and event features at global and local levels. With these modules, our framework can learn robust and discriminative representations for RE-VReID. Extensive experiments on three public RE-VReID benchmarks including EvReID, MARS and iLIDS-VID, demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method. The code is available at https://github.com/Reflection0427/Paths.

</details>


### [47] [RbFT-Net: Rectify-Before-Fuse Temporal Radar Anchors for 4D Radar-Camera Depth Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13102)
*Wentao Zhao,Shouxuan Wu,Yongtao Cen,Tianchen Deng,Yuyang Zhang,Jingchuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RbFT-Net 是一个端到端的“先校正后融合”框架，用于多帧 4D 雷达-相机深度补全，通过图像条件校正模块修正稀疏雷达帧的投影位置与度量深度，并选择性传播可靠锚点，从而抑制不可靠雷达测量的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达测量本身稀疏且易受杂波、多径反射和投影误差影响；聚合多帧虽能提供更密集的度量线索，但会引入时间失配与动态物体干扰。直接传播这类不可靠测量会大面积破坏预测深度图，因此需要在融合前对雷达测量进行校正并评估其可靠性。

Method: 提出 RbFT-Net：将累积雷达回波视为带噪声的时间锚点候选；设计图像条件校正模块，联合修正其图像平面位置与度量深度，并估计逐点可靠性；校正后的锚点先经选择性传播，再进行高层多模态融合，从而抑制不可靠测量的影响。

Result: 在 ZJU-4DRadarCam 和新采集的 4D 雷达-相机-激光雷达数据集上，RbFT-Net 一致优于所评估的独立雷达-相机方法，并与使用辅助单目深度模型的即插即用流水线保持竞争力；跨平台评估与组件分析进一步验证了校正与可靠性感知传播策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过“先校正后融合”及可靠性感知的选择性传播，RbFT-Net 能有效抑制不可靠雷达测量对深度补全的破坏性影响，在多个数据集上取得优越性能，证明了该策略的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: Dense metric depth prediction from cameras and millimeter-wave radar offers a cost-effective sensing solution for autonomous systems. However, radar measurements are inherently sparse and susceptible to clutter, multipath reflections, and projection errors. While aggregating multiple radar frames provides denser metric cues, it also introduces temporal misalignment and dynamic-object interference. Directly propagating such unreliable measurements can therefore corrupt large regions of the predicted depth map. To address this issue, we propose RbFT-Net, an end-to-end rectify-before-fuse framework for multi-frame 4D radar-camera depth completion. Rather than assuming accumulated radar returns to be accurate, RbFT-Net treats them as noisy temporal anchor candidates. An image-conditioned rectification module jointly corrects their image-plane locations and metric depths while estimating pointwise reliability. The rectified anchors are then selectively propagated before high-level multi-modal fusion, suppressing the influence of unreliable measurements. Experiments on ZJU-4DRadarCam and a newly collected 4D radar-camera-LiDAR dataset show that RbFT-Net consistently outperforms the evaluated independent radar-camera methods and remains competitive with plug-in pipelines using auxiliary monocular depth models. Cross-platform evaluation and component analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed rectification and reliability-aware propagation strategy.

</details>


### [48] [Online Learning of Correspondences between Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13104)
*Michael Felsberg,Fredrik Larsson,Johan Wiklund,Niclas Wadströmer,Jörgen Ahlberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Neyman卡方散度的迭代点对应学习方法，通过信道向量表示位置不确定性密度，在图像序列上实时、快速地收敛并取得高精度对应映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点对应问题通常依赖全局优化或透视投影下的基础矩阵，计算量大且假设受限。作者希望针对一般成像几何、仅有图像点集对而无法获取3D场景的情况，提供一种可迭代、实时且更优的对应映射求解方法。

Method: 将估计位置与真实位置的不确定性用概率密度表示，并用基函数方法将密度编码为信道向量；以Neyman卡方散度作为目标函数优化两个视图间的映射，并随每一对新图像迭代更新映射，从而实现快速收敛和高精度。

Result: 算法能够实时运行，在收敛速度和精度上均优于现有最先进方法，并在多个实验中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该迭代点对应学习方法在一般成像几何下无需3D信息即可实时高精度求解对应关系，相比基于全局优化或基础矩阵的方法更具优势。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for iterative learning of point correspondences between image sequences. Points moving on surfaces in 3D space are projected into two images. Given a point in either view, the considered problem is to determine the corresponding location in the other view. The geometry and distortions of the projections are unknown as is the shape of the surface. Given several pairs of point-sets but no access to the 3D scene, correspondence mappings can be found by excessive global optimization or by the fundamental matrix if a perspective projective model is assumed. However, an iterative solution on sequences of point-set pairs with general imaging geometry is preferable. We derive such a method that optimizes the mapping based on Neyman's chi-square divergence between the densities representing the uncertainties of the estimated and the actual locations. The densities are represented as channel vectors computed with a basis function approach. The mapping between these vectors is updated with each new pair of images such that fast convergence and high accuracy are achieved. The resulting algorithm runs in real-time and is superior to state-of-the-art methods in terms of convergence and accuracy in a number of experiments.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Physics-Faithful Generation of Scientific Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13112)
*Minghui Zhang,Jinxin Shi,Yifan Chang,Liangliang Zhao,Yuandong Pu,Qian Yu,Ming Hu,Hanxiao Zhang,Yun Gu,Yirong Chen,Yu Qiao,Bo Zhang,Xiangchao Yan,Bin Fu,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Princigram，一个物理上忠实可信的科学图表生成系统，核心创新是结构化物理思维链（SP-CoT）——一种按物理子学科固定模式分解图表推理的监督与提示方法，并配套构建了 430 万张物理图像的标注数据及 430 万/11.5 万专家级标注，以及可逐项检验物理事实的 VeriphyT2IBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型虽已具备照片级真实感，但在生成科学图表时不可靠——图表价值取决于物理正确性（力的方向、坐标系、热力学状态、方程与场景匹配等），而非外观。通用模型基于物理描述浅薄的网络图文训练，生成的图表"看起来合理但物理错误"，在教育与科学传播中有害。现有缺乏结构化物理监督与可分解的评估方式。

Method: 提出结构化物理思维链（SP-CoT）：一种按子学科划分的固定 schema，将物理图表分解为从场景识别、力/过程分析到支配定律与综合的显式多步推理链，横跨六个子学科。与自由形式思维链不同，SP-CoT 遵循固定 schema 和严格保真规则，将"视觉可见事实"与"物理推断推理"分离，并以符号化方式对所有数学进行类型化。它同时充当稠密训练监督（数据管道）和推理时的结构化"思考"提示（统一多模态骨干网络）。并构建 430 万物理图像的结构化标注数据集（含 11.5 万专家级标注）。此外提出 VeriphyT2IBench：其问题由每个留出图自身的结构化标注生成，每张图成为一个关于其对象、力、状态的逐项二元问题库，使评判模型得分可分解为具名的物理事实而非单一整体分数。

Result: 在 GenExam 的物理子集和 VeriphyT2IBench 上，Princigram 表明显式物理结构化监督能够提升生成科学图表的物理忠实度（正确的力方向、有效坐标系、一致的热力学状态、与场景匹配的方程等）。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化物理思维链（SP-CoT）作为稠密监督与结构化推理提示，辅以大规模结构化标注数据和逐项分解的评估基准 VeriphyT2IBench，Princigram 证明了物理结构化监督对提升科学图表生成物理忠实度的有效性，为教育及科学传播中的可靠图表生成提供了方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation has reached photorealistic quality, yet state-of-the-art systems remain unreliable at producing scientific diagrams, whose value depends not on appearance but on physical faithfulness: correct force directions, valid coordinate systems, consistent thermodynamic states, and equations matching the depicted scenario. Trained on web imagery with physically shallow captions, generic models produce diagrams that look plausible but are physically wrong, harmful in education and scientific communication. We present Princigram, a physics-faithful scientific-diagram generator, and its data pipeline. Our central advance is Structured Physical Chain-of-Thought (SP-CoT): a per-subdiscipline schema that decomposes a physics diagram into an explicit multi-step reasoning chain across six subdisciplines, from scene identification through force or process analysis to governing laws and synthesis. Unlike free-form chain-of-thought, SP-CoT follows a fixed schema with strict fidelity rules that separate visually grounded facts from physically inferred reasoning and type all mathematics symbolically; it serves both as dense training supervision and, at inference, as a structured "thinking" prompt. With it we curate and structurally annotate 4.3 million physics images, of which 115,037 carry expert-level annotation, and adapt a unified multimodal backbone. We further introduce VeriphyT2IBench, whose questions are derived from each held-out diagram's own structured annotation: each diagram becomes an item-specific bank of binary questions about its objects, forces, and states, so a judge model's score decomposes into named physical facts rather than one holistic number. On the physics subset of GenExam and on VeriphyT2IBench, Princigram shows that explicit physics-structured supervision improves the physical faithfulness of generated scientific diagrams.

</details>


### [50] [EgoMonth: A Month-Level Egocentric Video Benchmark for Long-Term Spatiotemporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13113)
*Weitao Chen,Hu Jiaxin,Xie Tianyidan,Yang Li,Yuyi Qian,Banghao Xu,Ziheng Tang,Shenyi Wang,Mingyue Yu,Duo Li,Jiacheng Shi,Gao Wang,Zhan Xu,Zhicheng Qiu,Xuanfu Li,Jian Yang,Lanjun Wang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个以"月"为时间尺度的第一视角视频理解基准 EgoMonth，包含20名参与者、300多小时、跨20至120天的日常录制及1,443道人工标注选择题，并通过三层认知框架（图式巩固、情节索引、级联推理）的14项任务评估现有多模态大模型，发现最强模型 Gemini 2.5 Pro 仅达71.8%宏平均准确率，远低于94.2%的人类基线，揭示当前 MLLM 是"有损摘要器"而非"忠实记忆器"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频基准主要依赖网络视频，缺乏片段间的时空连续性，难以检验模型能否在数天乃至数周的真实世界体验中保持一致的长期记忆，因此需要构建一个真正连续、长时程的自我中心（第一视角）视频评测基准。

Method: 构建 EgoMonth 基准：采集20名参与者20至120天、共300余小时的第一视角日常视频，人工标注1,443道多选题；设计基于认知的三层14任务评测框架——图式巩固（Schema Consolidation）、情节索引（Episodic Indexing）与级联推理（Cascading Reasoning）；在此框架上对开源与闭源的先进 MLLM 进行系统评测，并以经修正的人类表现作为基线对照。

Result: 最佳模型 Gemini 2.5 Pro 的宏平均准确率仅71.8%，比修正后的人类基线94.2%低22.4个百分点；多个模型在路线推理（Route Reasoning）、跨视角空间推理（Cross-view Spatial Reasoning）与方向判断（Direction Judgement）等任务上接近或低于25%的随机水平；即使最强的闭源模型也显著落后于人类。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型更像"有损摘要器"而非"忠实记忆器"，尚不具备长期、连续的时空记忆能力；研究指出需要设计具备真正长期时空记忆的新型架构，以支撑月级乃至更长时间尺度的真实世界视频理解。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to substantial progress in video understanding, accompanied by a growing number of long video benchmarks. However, existing benchmarks rely predominantly on web-sourced videos that lack inter-clip spatiotemporal continuity, making it difficult to assess whether models can maintain consistent memory across days or weeks of real-world experience. We introduce EgoMonth, the first month-level egocentric video understanding benchmark. EgoMonth comprises over 300 hours of first-person daily-life recordings from 20 participants spanning 20 to 120 days, paired with 1,443 human-crafted multiple-choice question-answer pairs. We design a cognitively grounded 14-task evaluation framework organized into three hierarchical cognitive levels: Schema Consolidation, Episodic Indexing, and Cascading Reasoning. Evaluation of state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source MLLMs reveals that even the best-performing model, Gemini 2.5 Pro, achieves only 71.8% macro-average accuracy, remaining 22.4 percentage points below the corrected human baseline of 94.2%. Several models perform near or below the 25% chance level on tasks such as Route Reasoning, Cross-view Spatial Reasoning, and Direction Judgement, while even the strongest closed-source model remains substantially below human performance. These results indicate that current MLLMs function as lossy summarizers rather than faithful memorizers, highlighting the need for architectures with genuine long-term spatiotemporal memory.

</details>


### [51] [Fast Iterative Five point Relative Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13114)
*Johan Hedborg,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 Powell's Dog Leg 算法的新迭代方法，用于求解两相机间相对位姿，速度约为 Nistér 五点算法的两倍，且精度相当，并可扩展至多于五点的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相机相对位姿的鲁棒估计是运动恢复结构（Structure and Motion）方法的基础。现有最先进的 Nistér 五点法虽速度快、配合 RANSAC 可提高精度，但其计算效率仍有提升空间，因此需要更快且保持同样精度的求解方法。

Method: 提出一种基于 Powell's Dog Leg 算法的新迭代方法，用于求解五点相对位姿问题；该方法可容易地扩展到五点以上并保持高效的误差度量，从而也适用于作为精化（refinement）步骤。

Result: 新方法与 Nistér 算法精度相同，但速度约为其两倍；并可在 RANSAC 方案下通过增加迭代次数提升精度。该方法已在三类具有已知真实值的（ground truth）数据集上进行了系统评测。

Conclusion: 基于 Powell's Dog Leg 的迭代方法在保持与 Nistér 算法同等精度的同时显著提升速度，且具备向多点扩展的能力，既适合作为主求解器，也适合作为精化步骤，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Robust estimation of the relative pose between two cameras is a fundamental part of Structure and Motion methods. For calibrated cameras, the five point method together with a robust estimator such as RANSAC gives the best result in most cases. The current state-of-the-art method for solving the relative pose problem from five points is due to Nister [9], because it is faster than other methods and in the RANSAC scheme one can improve precision by increasing the number of iterations. In this paper, we propose a new iterative method, which is based on Powell's Dog Leg algorithm. The new method has the same precision and is approximately twice as fast as Nister's algorithm. The proposed method is easily extended to more than five points while retaining a efficient error metrics. This makes it also very suitable as an refinement step. The proposed algorithm is systematically evaluated on three types of datasets with known ground truth.

</details>


### [52] [QuISE: Defense against Typographic Attacks on VLMs via Query-Irrelevant Semantic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13119)
*Shubin Lu,Jiaqi Yin,Yihao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuISE 是一种针对视觉语言模型的排版攻击防御方法，无需模型修改或训练，通过查询无关的语义编辑定位并替换图像中的误导性文本，再根据编辑后图像间答案的一致性得出最终结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排版攻击通过在图像中注入误导性文本，使视觉语言模型依赖对抗性文字线索而非视觉证据作出错误回答。现有防御大多需要修改模型、额外训练或访问模型内部组件，难以应用于现代闭源视觉语言模型。

Method: 提出 QuISE，一种与模型无关、无需训练的黑盒防御方法：首先通过"影响力感知文本定位"识别可能影响当前查询的文本区域，然后将这些区域替换为两种与查询和图像均无关但语义不同的替换文本，最后比较编辑后图像间的回答一致性来确定最终答案。

Result: 在三个排版攻击基准、四种攻击设置和四个视觉语言模型上的大量实验表明，QuISE 持续提升防御后的准确率：恢复率达 67.9%–75.0%，而损害率仅为 0.5%–1.1%。

Conclusion: QuISE 以模型无关、免训练的黑盒方式有效防御排版攻击，在保持较低副作用（损害率）的同时显著恢复视觉语言模型的正确回答能力，为闭源模型的安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Typographic attacks pose a critical threat to vision-language models (VLMs) by injecting misleading text into images and causing models to rely on adversarial textual cues rather than visual evidence. Existing defenses often require model-specific modifications, additional training, or access to internal model components, limiting their applicability to modern closed-source VLMs. In this paper, we propose QuISE, a model-agnostic, training-free black-box defense based on query-irrelevant semantic editing. QuISE first identifies text regions likely to affect the current query through influence-aware text localization. QuISE then replaces these regions with two semantically distinct replacement texts that are irrelevant to both the query and the image. The final answer is determined by answer consistency across the edited images. Extensive experiments on three typographic-attack benchmarks, four attack settings, and four VLMs show that QuISE consistently improves defended accuracy. QuISE achieves a recovery rate of 67.9-75.0% with a harm rate of 0.5-1.1%.

</details>


### [53] [Predicting Signed Distance Functions for Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13135)
*Emil Brissman,Joakim Johnander,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的实例分割思路：不依赖锚框，而是训练神经网络在每个像素沿不同方向预测到最近物体轮廓的距离图，通过聚合这些方向距离图近似符号距离函数（SDF），再经阈值化得到前景分割，并在COCO上以前景IoU优于YOLACT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例分割中物体形状不受约束，尤其对绳索等细长物体，锚框方法仅能提供有限的形状集合，若为所有可能形状定义合适的锚框会导致先验框数量不可行。因此需要一种能更好地捕捉真实世界多样形状、不依赖预定义锚框的分割方法。

Method: 训练神经网络在每个像素处预测沿不同方向到最近物体轮廓的距离，得到多张方向距离图；将这些距离图池化（聚合）后近似符号距离函数（SDF）；再对SDF进行阈值化，从而获得前景-背景分割。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，该分割方法在前景交并比（IoU）指标上优于当前最先进的实例分割方法YOLACT。但距离图虽包含个体实例信息，将其映射到完整的实例级分割并不直接，尚未完全解决实例分离问题。

Conclusion: 作者认为这种基于方向距离图/SDF的方法能更好地刻画真实世界中的多样形状，是一条有前景的实例分割研究方向，尽管从距离图到完整实例分割的映射仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Visual instance segmentation is a challenging problem and becomes even more difficult if objects of interest varies unconstrained in shape. Some objects are well described by a rectangle, however, this is hardly always the case. Consider for instance long, slender objects such as ropes. Anchor-based approaches classify predefined bounding boxes as either negative or positive and thus provide a limited set of shapes that can be handled. Defining anchor-boxes that fit well to all possible shapes leads to an infeasible number of prior boxes. We explore a different approach and propose to train a neural network to compute distance maps along different directions. The network is trained at each pixel to predict the distance to the closest object contour in a given direction. By pooling the distance maps we obtain an approximation to the signed distance function (SDF). The SDF may then be thresholded in order to obtain a foreground-background segmentation. We compare this segmentation to foreground segmentations obtained from the state-of-the-art instance segmentation method YOLACT. On the COCO dataset, our segmentation yields a higher performance in terms of foreground intersection over union (IoU). However, while the distance maps contain information on the individual instances, it is not straightforward to map them to the full instance segmentation. We still believe that this idea is a promising research direction for instance segmentation, as it better captures the different shapes found in the real world.

</details>


### [54] [MergeOver: Post-Training Token Merging for Recursive Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13141)
*Junseo Kim,Uraz Odyurt,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MergeOver，一种免训练的后处理方法，将 Token Merging（ToMe）整合进递归权重共享的 Sliced Recursive Transformer（SReT）中，以缓解其计算与内存开销，在边缘设备上恢复部分吞吐量和内存收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers（ViTs）虽性能优异，但参数量大、计算复杂度为二次方，难以部署到资源受限的边缘硬件。递归权重共享可降低参数量，Token Merging 可缓解计算与内存瓶颈，但将两者整合通常需要昂贵的重训练，这一交叉领域鲜有探索。

Method: 提出 MergeOver，一种后训练方法，将 ToMe 集成到递归权重共享的 SReT 中。通过 Unmerge 追踪栈、约束安全的合并率调整、以及跨空间置换的同步 token-mass 追踪，解决整合中的空间与合并约束。并采用逐阶段单次调度：在每个阶段的首个 block 执行 token 缩减，后续递归迭代保持固定序列长度。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上，所选配置的 top-1 精度下降 1.47 个百分点。GPU 上，batch size 1 和 16 时峰值激活内存分别降低 37.3% 和 38.4%；吞吐量在 batch size 1 时下降 21.7%，在 batch size 16 时提升 21.7%。在 Raspberry Pi 5（ARM CPU）上，batch size 1 和 16 时延迟分别降低 2.4% 和 17.6%。

Conclusion: MergeOver 能在无需重训练的情况下，恢复递归权重共享所引入的相当一部分吞吐量与内存成本，为将 Token Merging 与分层递归 Transformer 结合提供了基线。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) demonstrate exceptional performance in computer vision but suffer from large parameter counts and quadratic computational complexity, severely limiting their deployment on resource-constrained edge hardware. While recursive weight-sharing reduces parameter counts and token merging mitigates computational and memory bottlenecks, integrating these two paradigms without costly retraining is non-trivial, leaving this intersection largely unexplored. We propose MergeOver, a post-training approach that integrates Token Merging (ToMe) into the recursively weight-shared Sliced Recursive Transformer (SReT). Through an Unmerge tracking stack, constraint-safe merge-rate adjustment, and synchronised token-mass tracking across spatial permutations, MergeOver resolves the spatial and merging constraints of this integration. We further employ a stage-wise single-shot schedule that performs token reduction at the first block of each stage and maintains a fixed sequence length throughout its subsequent recursive iterations. Benchmarked on ImageNet-1K, our selected configuration reduces top-1 accuracy by 1.47 percentage points. On the GPU, it reduces peak activation memory by 37.3% and 38.4% at batch sizes 1 and 16, while throughput decreases by 21.7% at batch size 1 but increases by 21.7% at batch size 16. On a Raspberry Pi 5 (ARM CPU), it reduces latency by 2.4% and 17.6% at batch sizes 1 and 16. These results show that MergeOver can recover a meaningful part of the throughput and memory cost that recursive weight-sharing introduces, without retraining, and provides a baseline for combining token merging with hierarchical recursive transformers.

</details>


### [55] [Geometry-Grounded Unified 3D Perception for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13147)
*Longfei Xu,Xiaohui Wang,Zehao Huang,Han Li,Ya Yang,Naiyan Wang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoUP 框架，将 VGGT 的重建导向潜表示适配到多相机驾驶场景，通过自注意力、时间注意力和视图注意力分解交叉图像交互，并注入标定感知的 raymap 编码，从而构建保留度量几何的统一 3D 表示，用于深度估计、3D 检测和语义占据预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的自动驾驶感知框架多采用为语义识别预训练的骨干网络，3D 几何信息仅通过下游任务专用模块引入，导致共享表示无法保留显式的度量几何和一致的 3D 场景结构；同时多相机流之间缺乏几何一致的统一表示。

Method: 作者提出 Geometry-grounded Unified 3D Perception (GeoUP) 框架：将 VGGT 的重建导向潜表示适配到已标定的流式多相机驾驶场景；将交叉图像交互分解为自注意力、时间注意力和视图注意力，分别捕捉结构上不同的时间对应与跨视角对应；注入标定感知的 raymap 编码以提供度量尺度和相机几何信息；所得几何基础潜表示分别解码为面级（度量深度估计）、实例级（3D 目标检测）和体级（语义占据预测）三个读出头，并通过多任务多数据集联合训练利用异构标注。

Result: 在 nuScenes、Argoverse 2、Waymo、KITTI 和 DDAD 上的大量实验表明，GeoUP 在检测、占据预测和深度估计任务上均达到 SOTA 性能，并能跨不同传感器配置和感知范围泛化。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了几何基础（geometry-grounded）表示对于统一 3D 驾驶感知的有效性，表明保留显式度量几何和一致 3D 结构的共享表示能够显著提升多任务感知性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Camera-based autonomous driving perception requires a shared representation that preserves metric 3D structure across synchronized multi-camera streams. However, existing image-based frameworks often rely on backbones pretrained for semantic recognition, and introduce 3D geometry through downstream task-specific modules. As a result, their shared representations may fail to preserve explicit metric geometry and consistent 3D scene structure. In this paper, we present a Geometry-grounded Unified 3D Perception (GeoUP) framework that adapts the reconstruction-oriented latent of VGGT to calibrated, streaming multi-camera driving scenes. GeoUP factorizes cross-image interaction into self, temporal, and view attention to capture structurally distinct temporal and cross-view correspondences. It further injects calibration-aware raymap encodings to provide metric scale and camera geometry. The resulting geometry-grounded latent is decoded for metric depth estimation, 3D object detection, and semantic occupancy prediction, corresponding to surface-, instance-, and volume-level readouts of the same 3D scene. Through joint multi-task and multi-dataset training, GeoUP effectively leverages heterogeneous annotations and generalizes across diverse sensor configurations and perception ranges. Extensive experiments on nuScenes, Argoverse 2, Waymo, KITTI, and DDAD demonstrate that GeoUP achieves SOTA performance across detection, occupancy, and depth estimation. These results validate the effectiveness of geometry-grounded representations for unified 3D driving perception.

</details>


### [56] [Less Annotation, More Interpretation: Prior-Guided Concept Bottleneck Models for Interpretable Cancer Imaging Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13148)
*Baoqiang Ma,Kenneth Gilhuijs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种先验引导的混合概念瓶颈模型（hybrid CBM），通过有限标注 + 未标注数据上类条件概念分布匹配 + 概念到诊断头的先验初始化，大幅降低可解释癌症影像模型的概念标注负担；在0-20%标注区间显著提升概念AUC，同时保持接近黑盒的诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽能提升癌症影像诊断预测的透明度，但其依赖实例级概念标注，标注成本高、难以实际应用。作者希望缓解这一标注负担，使可解释模型在仅有少量概念标注的情况下仍能可靠工作。

Method: 提出先验引导的混合CBM，融合三部分：(1) 有限的概念标注用于监督；(2) 对未标注患者在类条件下的概念分布做匹配（class-conditional concept distribution matching）；(3) 对概念到诊断的分类头进行先验初始化。在CBIS-DDSM（乳腺肿块与钙化）和LIDC-IDRI（肺结节）数据集上，于0-100%概念标注范围内评估，并通过消融实验定位各组件贡献。

Result: 在临床相关的0-20%标注区间，混合CBM相较匹配的标准CBM持续提升平均概念AUC，同时诊断性能接近黑盒模型。10%标注时概念AUC：肿块从0.619升至0.741，钙化从0.650升至0.787，肺结节从0.597升至0.642。消融表明先验初始化是提升概念检测的主要因素，可能通过稳定概念到诊断头实现。零样本VLM仍不足以胜任细粒度肿瘤级概念预测。

Conclusion: 结构化先验能显著降低可解释癌症影像模型的标注负担，使有限标注下仍能获得良好的概念检测与诊断性能，为实际临床应用中可解释模型的部署提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) can improve the transparency of cancer image diagnostic prediction by expressing predictions through radiological concepts. However, their dependence on instance-level concept annotations limits practical applicability. We propose a prior-guided hybrid CBM that integrates limited concept annotations, class-conditional concept distribution matching on unannotated patients, and prior initialization of the concept-to-diagnosis head. We evaluate the method on CBIS-DDSM mammographic masses and calcifications and LIDC-IDRI pulmonary nodules across 0-100% concept annotation. In the clinically relevant 0-20% annotation regime, the hybrid CBM consistently improves mean concept AUC over a matched standard CBM, while maintaining diagnostic performance close to black-box models. At 10% annotation specifically, concept AUC increases from 0.619 to 0.741 for masses, from 0.650 to 0.787 for calcifications, and from 0.597 to 0.642 for pulmonary nodules. Ablation experiments identify prior initialization as the main component contributing to improved concept detection, likely by stabilizing the concept-to-diagnosis head. Zero-shot VLMs remain insufficient for reliable fine-grained tumor-level concept prediction. These findings suggest that structured priors can substantially reduce the annotation burden of interpretable cancer imaging models.

</details>


### [57] [Splat-based Metal Artifact Reduction in Cone-Beam CT via Polychromatic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13159)
*Kiseok Choi,Inchul Kim,Jaemin Cho,Hyeongjun Cho,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯溅射（Gaussian Splatting）的物理启发、自校准金属伪影校正方法，用于在锥束CT（CBCT）中减轻束硬化伪影，无需手动金属掩膜即可高效重建3D体积，并在合成与真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT在成像高衰减材料（如金属）时会产生严重的束硬化伪影，其根源是X射线的多色（polychromatic）特性，而传统单色重建算法无法正确处理。现有的神经表示方法虽能提升重建质量，但计算开销大、难以实际部署。

Method: 提出一种物理启发、自校准的金属伪影减少方法：将多色X射线投影模型、材料依赖的衰减曲线和系统响应建模集成到高斯溅射框架中；训练时联合优化重建参数与X射线光谱特性，无需手动金属掩膜或强先验假设。此外，构建了经蒙特卡洛X射线仿真工具箱验证的高保真合成CBCT数据集生成流程，并发布含严重金属伪影的新数据集。

Result: 这是首个基于splat的CBCT束硬化减少方法。在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在伪影抑制和重建精度上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过物理建模与高斯溅射结合，实现了高效、无掩膜、自校准的CBCT金属束硬化伪影校正，兼具计算效率和重建质量，并推动了相关数据集的公开与标准化。

Abstract: Cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) enables volumetric reconstruction from X-ray projections, but suffers from severe artifacts--especially beam hardening--when imaging materials with high attenuation such as metals. These artifacts arise from the polychromatic nature of X-rays and are not properly addressed by conventional monochromatic reconstruction algorithms. While recent neural representation-based methods offer improved reconstruction quality, they are computationally expensive and often impractical for deployment. We propose a novel physics-inspired, self-calibrating metal artifact reduction method that efficiently reconstructs 3D CBCT volumes while correcting beam hardening artifacts. Our method integrates a polychromatic X-ray projection model, material-dependent attenuation profiles, and system response modeling into a Gaussian Splatting framework. Unlike prior work, we eliminate the need for manual metal masks or strong prior assumptions, and we optimize both reconstruction parameters and X-ray spectral characteristics jointly during training. We further introduce a high-fidelity synthetic CBCT dataset generation pipeline validated on Monte-Carlo x-ray simulation toolbox and release new datasets with severe metal-induced artifacts to support the community. This is the first splat-based method for reducing beam hardening in CBCT. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in artifact suppression and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [TRAPSBench: Vision-Language Models Encode but Fail to Express Epistemic Restraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13167)
*Fnu Pramono,John Cai,Sourabh Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型能够内在识别何时需要弃权（不回答），却无法将其表达出来，瓶颈在输出阶段而非感知阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当视觉证据被遮挡或混乱时，模型应选择弃权而非强行作答。作者旨在探究 VLM 是否具备识别“不可判定性”的能力，以及更关键的——为何这种能力未能转化为弃权行为。

Method: 构建 TRAPSBench：一个程序化生成的视频基准，包含 1404 对匹配的物理问题对，每对中通过一处针对性修改使结果无法从视觉证据判定。提出新指标 PECS（惩罚式认知校准分数），要求模型在结果可知时正确作答、不可知时弃权。在 5 个家族 16 个 VLM 上评测，并用线性探针解码隐藏状态的可回答性，以及通过操控单一层“弃权方向”进行因果干预。在 Qwen、Gemma、LLaVA 三个开放权重家族上复现。

Result: 16 个 VLM 的自发弃权表现差，最高 PECS 仅 0.292。线性探针在多个物理领域上能以最高 0.91 AUROC 从隐藏状态解码可回答性；操控弃权方向能因果性地诱导或抑制弃权。模型对文本不可能性的检测能力约为对缺失视觉证据的 4 倍，表明该失败在视觉不确定性上比文本不确定性更突出。

Conclusion: VLM 内部已编码了弃权所需的信息但无法输出该行为，存在“表征-输出”鸿沟，弥合这一差距可能需要输出阶段的干预手段。

Abstract: When visual evidence is occluded or chaotic, models should abstain. In this paper, we show that Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can internally distinguish when abstention is required, but fail to express it anyway. We introduce TRAPSBench, a procedurally generated video benchmark of 1,404 matched physics pairs in which a single targeted change renders the outcome undeterminable from the visual evidence. Furthermore, we introduce Penalized Epistemic Calibration Score (PECS), a new robust metric that requires models to both answer correctly when the outcome is knowable, and abstain when the outcome is not. Across 16 VLMs spanning five families, spontaneous restraint is poor: the best PECS is 0.292. The bottleneck is expression, not perception: linear probes decode answerability from hidden states at up to 0.91 AUROC across physics domains; steering a single-layer void direction causally induces or suppresses abstention. Our results replicate across three open-weight families (Qwen, Gemma, LLaVA). The failure is also more pronounced in visual than textual uncertainty: models detect textual impossibility about 4x more readily than missing visual evidence. Closing this representation--output gap likely requires output-stage interventions.

</details>


### [59] [A Controlled Study of Self-Supervised Image and Video Pretraining under Limited Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13183)
*Brunó B. Englert,Gijs Dubbelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在匹配的数据、架构和计算预算下，对图像与视频自监督学习（SSL）目标进行了受控实验，发现 DINOv2 风格的预训练在资源受限条件下表现最佳；将 DINOv2 与 VideoMAE 等视频目标联合可提升图像分类与分割，但会损害视频跟踪与相机位姿估计，揭示了语义与几何表征学习之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型的预训练规模不可持续且可能不必要；在资源受限条件下获得有效模型具有重要价值。作者旨在系统理解 SSL 目标在数据、架构、算力受限时的行为规律。

Method: 在受控条件下开展图像与视频 SSL 目标的对比研究，统一匹配数据、架构和计算预算。比较了对比学习、重建、特征预测和扩散等目标，并评估了图像-视频联合训练的独立与联合形式，在一系列图像与视频理解任务上进行验证。

Result: DINOv2 风格的预训练在有限资源下持续提供最强的综合性能；将 DINOv2 与 VideoMAE 等视频 SSL 目标结合可显著提升图像分类和分割性能，但会降低视频跟踪与相机位姿估计性能，表明语义与几何表征学习之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 在资源受限场景下，结合图像与视频 SSL 目标是有益的，但需要开发更好的方法以平衡语义、时序和几何监督。

Abstract: Visual foundation models are a cornerstone of image and video understanding but typically require large amounts of data and computation. The current scale required for pretraining visual foundation models may be unsustainable or unnecessary, and significant benefits arise when effective models can be obtained with fewer resources. To better understand how self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives behave under resource constraints, we conduct a controlled study of image and video SSL objectives under matched data, architecture, and compute budgets. We compare contrastive, reconstruction, feature-prediction, and diffusion objectives and evaluate both standalone and jointly trained image-video SSL formulations across a diverse set of image and video understanding tasks. Our results show that DINOv2-style pretraining consistently provides the strongest overall performance under limited resources. Furthermore, combining DINOv2 with video SSL objectives such as VideoMAE substantially improves image classification and segmentation performance, but degrades video tracking and camera-pose estimation performance, revealing an important tradeoff between semantic and geometric representation learning. These findings suggest that combining image and video SSL objectives can be beneficial in resource-limited settings, while highlighting the need for improved methods that better balance semantic, temporal, and geometric supervision.

</details>


### [60] [SketchSense: Learning to Interpret Imperfect Sketch Guidance for Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13186)
*Zian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchSense 通过同步去噪相互作用的 RGB 与结构双流，并用双向注意力融合、短语级对齐和草图感知空间调节，来解释不完美的草图引导，从而提升图像修复质量与结构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实草图往往混合了可靠的全局意图与局部拥挤、错位、不完整或刻意非常规的笔触。现有方法要么把输入草图当作去噪全程的固定条件（假设所有笔触均匀可靠，容易把局部错误传播到整个生成过程），要么在 RGB 合成前先将其精炼成干净结构（在可用外观与语义上下文出现之前就必须解决歧义结构）。两者都无法在生成过程中动态地分辨哪些笔触该保留、哪些该纠正。

Method: 提出 SketchSense 框架：1) 同步去噪相互作用的 RGB 流与结构流；2) 双向注意力融合（Bidirectional Attention Fusion）耦合外观生成与结构恢复，产出可反映模型当前草图解释的精炼结构；3) 短语级目标（phrase-level objective）对齐两流的语义接地；4) 草图感知空间调节（Sketch-Aware Spatial Regulation）根据局部生成状态调制注意力与融合过程；5) 可选的有符号先验将“保留 vs 纠正”意图注入特征表示与注意力行为。

Result: 在自然图像与结构复杂图像上的实验表明，SketchSense 在恢复质量与结构保真度两方面相较现有方法均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过同步去噪的双流交互与动态草图调节机制，SketchSense 有效解决了不完美草图中可靠与不可靠笔触混杂的问题，克服了“固定条件”与“先精炼后合成”两类范式的局限，在修复质量和结构保真度上实现大幅超越。

Abstract: Sketch-guided image inpainting provides intuitive structural control, yet real sketches often mix reliable global intent with locally crowded, displaced, incomplete, or deliberately unconventional strokes. Existing approaches typically either retain the input sketch as a fixed condition throughout denoising or refine it into a clean structure before RGB synthesis. The former assumes uniformly reliable strokes and can propagate local errors throughout generation; the latter must resolve ambiguous structure before emerging appearance and semantic context become available. We propose SketchSense, a framework that interprets imperfect sketch guidance by synchronously denoising interacting RGB and structure streams. Bidirectional Attention Fusion couples appearance generation with structural recovery, producing a refined structure that exposes the model's evolving sketch interpretation. A phrase-level objective aligns the semantic grounding of the two streams. Sketch-Aware Spatial Regulation further adapts sketch use to local generation states by modulating attention and the fusion process, while an optional signed prior injects preserve-versus-correct intent into feature representations and attention behavior. Experiments on natural and structurally complex imagery show substantial gains over existing methods in both restoration quality and structural fidelity.

</details>


### [61] [Fidelity-Constrained Anchoring for Black-Box Denoisers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13194)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保真度约束框架，通过将黑盒去噪器输出与输入做线性混合并选取满足局部保真约束的最大混合因子，实现无需重训练、计算量小的去噪结果锚定；其中PSNR约束有闭式解，SSIM约束通过逆SSIM结合迭代求根高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒去噪器输出可能过度偏离输入、丧失保真度，而去噪器内部不可修改或无法重训练；需要一个不改变去噪器本身、仅通过后处理即可精确控制输出与输入之间保真度的通用锚定机制。

Method: 将去噪图像与输入图像做线性混合，选择满足预设局部保真约束（PSNR或SSIM）的最大混合因子。在局部恒定混合假设下，PSNR约束可得到闭式解；对SSIM约束则基于逆SSIM导出可计算形式，并用迭代求根方法高效求解。

Result: 在DIV2K图像、合成高斯噪声、Real-ESRGAN和非局部均值去噪器的实验表明，锚定策略能有效实现保真控制，并在去噪性能与统计自然性（以残差噪声的超额峰度衡量）之间取得平衡；SSIM锚定在不同噪声水平下表现出比PSNR锚定更一致的行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的保真约束锚定框架无需重训练、附加计算少，能有效约束黑盒去噪器输出的保真度，兼顾去噪效果与噪声统计自然性；SSIM约束相比PSNR约束在跨噪声水平的稳定性上更具优势。

Abstract: We propose a fidelity-constrained framework that anchors the output of a black-box denoiser to its input without retraining and with little additional computation. The method linearly blends the denoised image with the input and selects the maximum blending factor that satisfies a prescribed local fidelity constraint using Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) or Structural Similarity Index (SSIM). For PSNR control, a closed-form solution is obtained under a local constant-blending assumption. For SSIM control, we derive a tractable formulation based on inverse SSIM under the same assumption and solve it efficiently using iterative root finding. Experiments on DIV2K images with synthetic Gaussian noise and outputs from Real-ESRGAN and a non-local means denoiser show that the proposed anchoring strategy provides effective fidelity control while balancing denoising performance and statistical naturalness, as measured by the excess kurtosis of residual noise. In particular, SSIM-based anchoring yields more consistent behavior across noise levels than PSNR-based anchoring.

</details>


### [62] [HPSD: Hybrid-Policy Self-Distillation for Text-Image-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13205)
*Jiazi Bu,Pengyang Ling,Yujie Zhou,Yibin Wang,Yuhang Zang,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Tianyi Wei,Xiaohang Zhan,Jiaqi Wang,Tong Wu,Dahua Lin,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略自蒸馏（HPSD）框架，让单个 TI2V 模型同时充当教师和学生：教师以高质量首帧和增强提示运行在 TI2V 模式，学生以普通提示运行在基础 T2V 模式，通过继承教师轨迹点并在自生成 rollout 上施加速度级监督，将特权条件的生成能力内部化到模型基础能力中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TI2V 模型在优质首帧或详细提示等特权条件下能显著提升视觉质量，但如何把这种特权条件激发的能力内化为模型自身的基础生成能力仍待解决。现有自蒸馏方法存在缺陷：简单的监督微调属于离策略（off-policy），监督局限于固定离线分布的教师终点而非学生访问的状态，缺乏针对演化策略的精确纠正；而近期在线策略（on-policy）蒸馏则存在条件-状态不匹配问题，监督被引向给定首帧而非学生实际内容，导致纠正误导。

Method: 提出混合策略自蒸馏（HPSD）框架：单个 TI2V 模型在不同条件下分别充当教师与学生——教师运行于 TI2V 模式并使用高质量首帧和增强提示，学生运行于基础 T2V 模式且仅使用原始提示。学生继承离策略教师轨迹点作为锚点，在本地将其向自身策略细化，最终在这些自生成的 rollout 上接受速度级别（velocity-level）的监督。

Result: 大量实验表明，HPSD 显著提升了 T2V 性能，同时带来可观的 TI2V 增益，有效增强了模型的基础生成能力。

Conclusion: HPSD 通过吸收教师特权先验同时保持精确的策略纠正，成功将特权条件所激发的能力内部化为模型基础生成能力，为 TI2V 模型的自蒸馏提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Text-Image-to-Video (TI2V) models are an emerging unified architecture, where a single model simultaneously supports text-to-video (T2V) and image-to-video (I2V) generation. Given a high-quality first frame or a detailed textual prompt, TI2V models unlock substantially better visual quality than their T2V mode, raising a natural question: can the capability elicited by such privileged conditions be internalized into the model's own base generation ability? A common approach toward this goal is model self-distillation. However, the most straightforward solution, supervised fine-tuning, follows an off-policy strategy: its supervision is confined to teacher-generated endpoints from a fixed offline distribution rather than student-visited states, lacking precise correction tailored to the evolving policy. Recent on-policy distillation methods instead suffer from condition-state mismatch, where supervision is steered toward the given first frame instead of the student's actual content, misleading the correction. To achieve self-distillation that absorbs the teacher's privileged prior while retaining precise policy correction, in this work, we propose Hybrid-Policy Self-Distillation (HPSD), a novel self-distillation framework where a single TI2V model acts as both teacher and student under different conditions: the teacher operates in TI2V mode with a high-quality first frame and an enhanced prompt, while the student runs in the base T2V mode with only the vanilla prompt. Specifically, the student inherits off-policy teacher trajectory points as anchors, locally refines them toward its own policy, and finally receives velocity-level supervision on these self-generated roll-outs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HPSD significantly improves T2V performance while also delivering notable TI2V gains, effectively strengthening the model's base generation ability.

</details>


### [63] [NARU: A Benchmark for NARrative Evolution and Cultural Nuance Understanding in Japanese Extreme Long Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13210)
*Yuheng Huang,Jianlang Chen,Jiayang Song,Hua Qi,Aza Kai,Vincent Markert,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Jianjun Zhao,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NARU 基准，用于评测日语长视频中的叙事演化与文化理解推理能力，包含 1,481 道题目、155 个视频共 146.8 小时，并在 8 种模型配置上评估，揭示现有 MLLM 在长程叙事整合与文化推理方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解基准很少联合评测"追踪演进叙事"与"解读隐含社会意义"这两类能力，尤其缺乏高语境、非英语媒体的评测。为填补这一空白，作者引入 NARU 基准。

Method: NARU 包含 1,481 道题、155 个视频（共 146.8 小时），覆盖 4 个叙事维度与 5 个文化维度。采用分层记忆式标注管线（hierarchical memory-based annotation pipeline），先将原始视频转化为结构化的事件、叙事与文化标注，再通过面向任务的合成与迭代式捷径去除（shortcut removal）生成问题；构建过程包含两个母语者验证阶段，共 68 名标注者参与。

Result: 在 8 种模型配置上的评测显示，模型在长程叙事整合与文化根基推理两方面均存在显著局限，暴露出持续的能力缺口。

Conclusion: NARU 提供了一个系统化的测试平台，旨在推动能够可靠解读长时、高语境视频的 MLLM 的发展。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding encompasses tasks that go beyond retrieving isolated events, including tracking an evolving narrative and interpreting social meaning that may remain implicit. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate these capabilities jointly, particularly in high-context, non-English media. To address this gap, we introduce NARU, a benchmark designed to evaluate Narrative evolution and Reasoning on cultural Understanding in Japanese long-form video. NARU consists of 1,481 questions grounded in 155 videos totaling 146.8 hours, spanning four narrative and five cultural dimensions. To construct the benchmark at this scale, we propose a hierarchical memory-based annotation pipeline that transforms raw video into structured event, narrative, and cultural annotations, then generates questions via task-oriented synthesis and iterative shortcut removal. The construction process includes two native-speaker verification stages involving 68 annotators. Evaluations across eight model configurations reveal substantial limitations in both long-range narrative integration and culturally grounded reasoning. By exposing these persistent gaps, NARU offers a systematic testing ground for developing MLLMs capable of reliably interpreting long-form, high-context video.

</details>


### [64] [UniCon-Former: Unified Convolution Transformer is All You Need for Hand Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13217)
*Mallika Garg,Debashis Ghosh,Pyari Mohan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一模型 UniCon-Former，将 CNN 的局部特征提取能力与 Transformer 的全局依赖建模能力相结合，以较低的计算开销在动态手势识别任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN 受限于感受野，难以有效捕获全局上下文；而 Transformer 虽能通过自注意力建模全局依赖，却存在冗余度高、计算开销大的问题。动态手势识别既需要局部细节又需要全局时序空间关系，因此需要一种能够兼取两者优势的统一架构。

Method: 提出统一模型 UniCon-Former，在每个 Transformer 阶段开头使用卷积投影来降低输入向量维度，从而形成金字塔结构，使模型同时学习局部与全局特征，并支持多尺度、高分辨率特征学习，减少资源占用。

Result: 在 NVGesture 和 Briareo 两个数据集上进行实验，UniCon-Former 以更少的参数量和 MACs（乘加运算次数）取得了 state-of-the-art 的结果。

Conclusion: UniCon-Former 成功融合 CNN 与 Transformer 的优势，在动态手势识别上实现了性能与效率的平衡，验证了卷积投影与金字塔结构设计在降低冗余和计算成本方面的有效性。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) capture local features efficiently but struggle with global context due to their limited receptive field. On the other hand, transformers effectively capture global dependencies through self-attention but suffer from high redundancy and computational costs. Thus, to leverage the advantages of both CNNs and transformers, we propose a unified model (UniCon-Former) that aims to provide robust and efficient performance on dynamic hand gesture recognition. The unified approach helps the model to learn both local and global features. At the beginning of each transformer stage, the convolution projections help in decreasing the dimension of the input vectors of the transformer block. This creates a pyramidal structure at each transformer stage. These features enable the UniCon-Former to reduce resource usage than vanilla transformers, making it flexible for learning multi-scale and high-resolution features, which is required in hand gesture recognition. We have performed experiments with NVGesture and Briareo datasets and achieved state-of-the-art results with fewer parameters and MACs.

</details>


### [65] [Reliability analysis for BraTS-GoAT segmentation: a controlled robustness study of deep-ensemble uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13223)
*Riya Deepak Shet,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在 BraTS-GoAT（Task 3）泛化性任务上，对脑肿瘤分割的 5 折交叉验证 nnU-Net 基线与 3 种子深度集成进行可靠性与不确定性评估，发现集成成员间的分歧在分布偏移下比单模型置信度更能指示错误，但其逐体素错误定位能力随偏移加剧而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在分布内能准确分割脑肿瘤，但当输入偏离训练数据时可能静默失败，这种风险对临床部署至关重要。作者不仅关注分割精度，更关注模型的不确定性是否"知道"自己错了，即在分布偏移下能否可靠地进行校准和错误检测。

Method: 在 BraTS-GoAT（Task 3）上训练一个 5 折交叉验证的 nnU-Net 基线（每个病例一个留出预测）和一个 3 种子的深度集成，在按区域的相关掩码上逐病例聚合地评估校准与错误检测性能；并开展受控鲁棒性研究，以分级合成损坏作为采集偏移的代理，考察单模型置信度与集成成员间分歧在干净与损坏条件下的行为差异。

Result: 分布内 3 种子集成较单一模型在相同留出划分上提升有限，校准改善最明显；在偏移下差异显现——单模型置信度持平而准确率和校准退化，集成成员间分歧则急剧上升（比干净条件高约四分之一到三分之一，是单模型响应的数倍）。官方验证榜上 5 折集成全肿瘤 Dice 达 0.87，泛化差距集中在较难区域，典型失败是漏检未见队列中的小卫星病灶。合成研究中，3 种子成员间的分歧作为采集偏移的案例级指标比单模型置信度更敏感，但其逐体素错误定位随严重程度增加而减弱。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是提供一项严谨、诚实的可靠性对比，而非宣称某种不确定性方法占优。结论表明集成成员间分歧在案例层面是更敏感的采集偏移指示器，但逐体素错误定位能力有限，提示可靠性评估需结合具体任务场景综合考量。

Abstract: Deep networks segment brain tumours accurately in-distribution, but can fail silently when the input differs from their training data. That risk is central to clinical deployment and is the premise of the BraTS-GoAT generalizability task. We ask not only how well a model segments, but whether its uncertainty knows when it is wrong. On BraTS-GoAT (Task 3) we train a 5-fold cross-validated nnU-Net baseline (one held-out prediction per case) and a 3-seed deep ensemble. Both are evaluated for calibration and error detection on a per-region relevant mask, aggregated per case. In-distribution the 3-seed ensemble improves modestly over the already strong single model on the same held-out split, with the clearest gain in calibration. The separation appears under shift. In a controlled robustness study using graded synthetic corruptions as a proxy for acquisition shift, the single model's confidence stays flat while its accuracy and calibration degrade. Inter-member disagreement instead rises steeply, about a quarter to a third above the clean condition, several times the single model's response. On the official validation leaderboard the 5-fold ensemble of those folds attains whole-tumour Dice 0.87. The generalization gap is concentrated on the harder regions, with a characteristic failure of missing small, satellite lesions on unseen cohorts. In the synthetic study, disagreement among the 3-seed members is a more sensitive case-level indicator of acquisition shift than single-model confidence. Its per-voxel error localisation weakens as severity grows. The contribution is a rigorous, honest reliability comparison rather than a claim that any one uncertainty method dominates.

</details>


### [66] [CoverPrune: Coverage-Driven Token Pruning for 3D VLMs via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13226)
*Peng Ling,Yingda Yin,Lingting Zhu,Weikai Chen,Shengju Qian,Zeyu Hu,Xin Wang,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoverPrune，一个无需训练的3D VLM推理时Token剪枝框架，将剪枝建模为最优传输问题，通过保留视觉证据覆盖而非追求多样性，在激进剪枝下维持空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D VLM推理面临巨量视觉Token导致的计算瓶颈；现有基于多样性的剪枝方法在3D环境中会丢弃代表性原型Token、保留离群点，破坏多视图一致性和几何结构，损害空间推理能力。

Method: 提出CoverPrune将推理时Token剪枝形式化为最优传输(OT)问题；设计特征-空间-时间(FST)传输代价与目标容量，并提出空间引导贪心选择(SGS)算法近似OT目标以规避不可解的连续子集选择问题；进一步提出CoverPrune-Lite加速变体，利用空间结构化局部匹配以极低开销实现剪枝。

Result: 在多个3D视觉-空间推理基准上的大量实验表明，CoverPrune达到最先进的Token效率，在高度激进的剪枝预算下仍保持稳健的推理性能。

Conclusion: 通过从"最大化多样性"转向"保留视觉证据覆盖"的范式转变，CoverPrune有效解决了3D VLM的Token剪枝瓶颈，兼顾效率与空间推理准确性，并提供轻量级加速版本。

Abstract: While 3D Vision-Language Models (3D VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable spatial reasoning capabilities, they suffer from massive visual token counts that create severe computational bottlenecks during inference. Existing token pruning methods primarily rely on diversity-based selection, discarding similar tokens to maximize dispersion. However, in 3D environments, this approach frequently drops representative prototype tokens in favor of outliers, breaking the multi-view consistencies and geometric structures essential for spatial reasoning. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift for 3D VLM token pruning: from maximizing diversity to preserving visual evidence coverage. We introduce CoverPrune, a training-free framework that formulates inference-time token pruning as an Optimal Transport (OT) problem. To overcome the intractable combinatorial subset selection inherent in this formulation, we design the Feature-Spatial-Temporal (FST) transport cost and target capacity, along with an efficient Spatial-Guided Greedy Selection (SGS) algorithm to approximate the OT objective. Furthermore, we propose CoverPrune-Lite, an accelerated variant utilizing spatially structured local matching for minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple 3D visual-spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our methods achieve state-of-the-art token efficiency, maintaining robust reasoning performance even under highly aggressive pruning budgets. Visit our project website at https://github.com/Brucess/CoverPrune.

</details>


### [67] [Reasoning for Social Audio-Visual Question Answering: Where Do We Stand?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13239)
*Koen P. de Vries,Xavier Alameda-Pineda,Estefanía Talavera,Stéphane Lathuilière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态大语言模型在音视频社交理解中普遍采用的思维链(CoT)方法提出质疑:发现基准IntentBench噪声严重,现有推理方法昂贵却并不比简单监督微调(Vanilla SFT)更好,且仅靠文本模态即可学到大量先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动多模态大语言模型(MLLM)实现具身社交智能是重要方向,而以CoT为代表的推理方法成为主流。作者希望检验这一主流路径:相关基准是否可靠、推理方法是否真正有效,以及视频模态在社交理解任务中是否被真正利用。

Method: 以HumanOmniV2及其IntentBench基准为参照,进行三方面的实证分析:1)审计IntentBench数据质量,剔除坏题和无视频即可答的平凡题,发布清洗后的IntentBench-Prime;2)在三个基准上对比Vanilla SFT与现有CoT等推理方法的效果与成本;3)分析仅用文本模态/文本字幕(替代视频)训练时模型性能与Vanilla SFT的差异,以量化跨模态先验的作用。

Result: 得到三项发现:1)IntentBench高度含噪,约7%的问题损坏,约23%的问题无需视频即可平凡作答,已清理并发布IntentBench-Prime;2)现有推理方法成本高昂却出人意料地无效,简单Vanilla SFT基线在三个基准上匹配或超越现有推理方法,且成本极低;3)大量先验可仅从文本模态学到,用文本字幕替代视频输入可获得与Vanilla SFT相当的性能。

Conclusion: 这些出人意料的发现揭示了当前MLLM在社交理解任务上的局限:基准噪声、推理方法低效、以及模型对视频模态的实际利用不足。作者公开IntentBench-Prime、Vanilla SFT模型与代码,并将Vanilla SFT确立为评估新型微调技术的必要基线。

Abstract: Training Multimodal Large Language Models for audio-visual social understanding is a crucial step toward embodied social intelligence.
